For a desired tolerance $\epsilon\in(0,1)$ , if $L\rho_{n}>\epsilon$ , we replace $\tau$ by $\frac{\tau}{2}$ , update $\mu=\frac{\tau}{4(\theta-1)}$ , and recompute $L_{f}$ , $L$ , and $\rho_{n}$ .
This procedure terminates once $L\rho_{n}\leq\epsilon$ .
Consequently, every generated instance satisfies the sufficient global co-hypomonotonicity condition and the stronger numerical requirement $L\rho_{n}\leq\epsilon<1$ .
In this case, Assumption 1 also holds.
$\mathrm{(v)}$ Sparse stationary point.
For each problem instance, we sample an active set $\mathcal{S}\subseteq\{1,\cdots,m\}$ uniformly such that $j_{1},j_{2}\in\mathcal{S}$ (i.e., we uniformly sample $\tilde{\mathcal{S}}\subseteq\{1,\cdots,m\}\setminus\{j_{1},j_{2}\}$ of cardinality $|\tilde{\mathcal{S}}|=|\mathcal{S}|-2$ , then define $\mathcal{S}:=\tilde{\mathcal{S}}\cup\{j_{1},j_{2}\}$ ).
We choose $z_{j}^{\star}:=\frac{\theta+1}{2}\lambda\mathbf{1}_{\{j\in\mathcal{S}\}}$ for all $j\in[m]$ .
Hence, all active coordinates satisfy $\lambda
$\mathrm{(vi)}$ Nonmonotonicity verification.
Since $g_{i}^{\top}d_{\mathrm{sw}}=0$ for every $i$ , the Hessian of $f$ satisfies $H_{f}d_{\mathrm{sw}}=\mu d_{\mathrm{sw}}$ .
Choose $\varepsilon\in\big(0,\frac{(\theta-1)\lambda}{\sqrt{2}}\big)$ arbitrarily, and define $z^{\varepsilon}:=z^{\star}+\varepsilon d_{\mathrm{sw}}$ and
$x^{\varepsilon}:=[z^{\varepsilon},y^{\star}]$ .
Then, the two perturbed coordinates $z_{j_{1}}^{\varepsilon}=z_{j_{1}}^{\star}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}$ and $z_{j_{2}}^{\varepsilon}=z_{j_{2}}^{\star}-\frac{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}$ remain strictly in the middle SCAD region $(\lambda,\theta\lambda)$ .
Define $w^{\star}:=Gx^{\star}+[\xi^{\star},0]$ and $w^{\varepsilon}:=Gx^{\varepsilon}+[\xi^{\varepsilon},0]$ .
It is straightforward to verify that $\xi^{\varepsilon}=\xi^{\star}=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\tau\lambda\mathbf{1}_{\{j\in\mathcal{S}\}}e_{j}$ satisfy $\xi^{\varepsilon}\in\tau\lambda\partial\|z^{\varepsilon}\|_{1}+\mathcal{N}_{\mathcal{Z}}(z^{\varepsilon})$ and $\xi^{\star}\in\tau\lambda\partial\|z^{\star}\|_{1}+\mathcal{N}_{\mathcal{Z}}(z^{\star})$ .
Then, similar to (108), we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w^{\varepsilon}-w^{\star},x^{\varepsilon}-x^{\star}\rangle&=&\langle\nabla f(z^{\varepsilon})-\nabla f(z^{\star}),z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}\rangle-\tau\langle\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\varepsilon})-\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\star}),z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\langle\xi^{\varepsilon}-\xi^{\star},z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}\rangle+\langle\nabla h(y^{\star})-\nabla h(y^{\star}),y^{\star}-y^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}$
The last two terms on the right-hand side are zero.
Moreover, using the gradient of $\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}$ , $z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}=\varepsilon d_{\mathrm{sw}}$ , and $g_{i}^{\top}d_{\mathrm{sw}}=0$ for all $i$ , we obtain
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\varepsilon})-\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\star})&=&\frac{1}{\theta-1}(z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}),\quad\text{and}\\
\nabla f(z^{\varepsilon})-\nabla f(z^{\star})&=&\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}g_{i}^{\top}+\mu\mathbb{I}\big)(z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon g_{i}(g_{i}^{\top}d_{\mathrm{sw}})+\mu(z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star})=\mu(z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}).\end{array}$
Therefore, we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w^{\varepsilon}-w^{\star},x^{\varepsilon}-x^{\star}\rangle&=&\big(\mu-\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\big)\|z^{\varepsilon}-z^{\star}\|^{2}<0\end{array}$
due to the choice $\mu=\frac{\tau}{4(\theta-1)}<\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}$ .
Since $\varepsilon$ can be chosen arbitrarily small, the last inequality shows that $\Phi$ fails to be monotone in every neighborhood of $x^{\star}$ .
$\mathrm{(c)}$ Experiment setup and algorithms.
Our setup is characterized by a tuple $(n,m,T)$ , with two experiments: $(n,m,T)=(1000,200,20)$ , leading to $p=420$ , and $(n,m,T)=(2000,460,30)$ , leading to $p=950$ .
For each setting, we generate 10 random problem instances as described above.
We test four variants of our (VAPEG) and compare them with the same three benchmarks as before.
The parameters are chosen as in Subsection 6.1.
For all algorithms, the initial point is chosen as $x^{0}=[z^{0},\mathbf{0}]$ , where $z^{0}$ is sampled i.i.d. from $\mathcal{U}(1.1\lambda,0.9\theta\lambda)$ , so that every portfolio component lies strictly inside the concave region of the SCAD penalty.
$\mathrm{(d)}$ Numerical results.
The results of these experiments are reported in Figure 6.
Figure 6:
The performance of the seven algorithms for solving the soft-robust bond-portfolio construction problem (91) in two experiments, averaged over $10$ problem instances for each.
Here, $\mathcal{G}_{\eta}$ is the FBS residual defined by (26).
Unlike the bilinear matrix game problem considered in Subsection 6.1, whose associated operator is monotone, the soft-robust bond-portfolio problem is genuinely nonmonotone due to the concave part of the SCAD penalty.
Moreover, by construction, the problem remains nonmonotone around the stationary point.
Nevertheless, the performance of the algorithms remains largely consistent across the two experiments.
In both experiments, VAPEG-Saga and VAPEG-Sarah converge substantially faster and attain significantly higher accuracy than the other methods, reaching relative residuals of approximately $10^{-8}$ , with VAPEG-Sarah performing slightly better than VAPEG-Saga.
VAPEG-Svrg also performs relatively well, reaching a final residual of roughly $10^{-6}$ , whereas
VAPEG-Sgd also reaches the magnitude of $10^{-6}$ .
Meanwhile, OG converges more slowly, and the two benchmark methods, VrFRBS and VrEG, exhibit the slowest residual decay.
These results suggest that the advantages of the proposed variance-reduced schemes persist beyond the monotone setting and extend to structured nonmonotone finite-sum inclusions.

Appendix A Preliminary Results and Example Verification.
This appendix provides useful lemmas.
It also verifies the examples in Subsection 2.4.
A.1 Useful lemmas.
We recall some useful results which will be used in our analysis.
Lemma A.1 ([11], Lemma 5.31)
Let $\{u_{k}\}$ , $\{v_{k}\}$ , $\{\gamma_{k}\}$ , and $\{\varepsilon_{k}\}$ be nonnegative sequences such that $A_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\gamma_{k}<+\infty$ and $B_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\varepsilon_{k}<+\infty$ .
In addition, we assume that
$u_{k+1}\leq(1+\gamma_{k})u_{k}-v_{k}+\varepsilon_{k},\quad\textrm{for all $k\geq 0$}.$
(93)
Then, we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}u_{k}$ exists and $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}v_{k}<+\infty$ .
Lemma A.2 ([102])
Given a nonnegative sequence $\{u_{k}\}$ and $\omega\geq 0$ such that $\lim_{k\to\infty}k^{\omega+1}u^{k}$ exists and $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}k^{\omega}u_{k}<+\infty$ .
Then, we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}k^{\omega+1}u^{k}=0$ .
We also need the well-known Robbins-Siegmund supermartingale theorem [93].
Lemma A.3 ([93])
Let $\{U_{k}\}$ , $\{\alpha_{k}\}$ , $\{V_{k}\}$ and $\{R_{k}\}$ be sequences of nonnegative integrable random variables on some arbitrary probability space and adapted to the filtration $\left\{\mathcal{F}_{k}\right\}$ with $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\alpha_{k}<+\infty$ and $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}R_{k}<+\infty$ almost surely, and
$\mathbb{E}\big[U_{k+1}\mid\mathcal{F}_{k}\big]\leq(1+\alpha_{k})U_{k}-V_{k}+R_{k},$
(94)
almost surely for all $k\geq 0$ .
Then, almost surely, $\{U_{k}\}$ converges to a nonnegative random variable $U$ and $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}V_{k}<+\infty$ almost surely.
The following lemma is proven similarly to [30, Theorem 3.2.] and [33, Proposition 4.1.].
We recall it here without repeating the proof to cover the composite mapping $\Phi$ in (GE).
Lemma A.4
Suppose that $\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)$ in (GE) is closed and $\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)\neq\emptyset$ .
Let $\{x^{k}\}$ be a sequence of random vectors such that for all $x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)$ , $\{\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\}$ almost surely converges to a $[0,\infty)$ -valued random variable.
In addition, assume that $\{\|w^{k}\|\}$ for $(x^{k},w^{k})\in\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)$ also almost surely converges to zero.
Then, $\{x^{k}\}$ almost surely converges to a $\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)$ -valued random variable $x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)$ .
A.2 Verifying Example 3 in Subsection 2.3
We verify the co-hypomonotonicity of the operator in Example 3 from Subsection 2.3.
For any $x,y\in\mathrm{dom}(A)$ , let $w_{x}\in Ax$ and $w_{y}\in Ay$ .
Then, $u:=-\rho^{-1}x-w_{x}\in Tx$ and $v:=-\rho^{-1}y-w_{y}\in Ty$ .
Therefore, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle u-v,x-y\rangle&=&-\rho^{-1}\|x-y\|^{2}-\langle w_{x}-w_{y},x-y\rangle,\\
\rho\|u-v\|^{2}&=&\rho\|\rho^{-1}(x-y)+(w_{x}-w_{y})\|^{2}=\rho^{-1}\|x-y\|^{2}+2\langle w_{x}-w_{y},x-y\rangle+\rho\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Adding these two identities gives
$\langle u-v,x-y\rangle+\rho\|u-v\|^{2}=\langle w_{x}-w_{y},x-y\rangle+\rho\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}\geq 0$ ,
where the inequality follows from the monotonicity of $A$ and $\rho>0$ .
Consequently, $\langle u-v,x-y\rangle\geq-\rho\|u-v\|^{2}$ for all $(x,u),(y,v)\in\mathrm{gra}(T)$ , showing that $T$ is $\rho$ -co-hypomonotone.
$\square$
A.3 Verifying examples in Subsection 2.4.
We present more details of Subsection 2.4.
Example 2.
We provide a more detailed explanation of Example 2 in Subsection 2.4.
First, using Jensen’s inequality, it is easy to check that $G$ satisfies (3) in Assumption 1 with $\alpha=1$ .
Second, for all $x,y\in\mathrm{dom}(\Phi)$ , let $w_{x}:=Gx+v_{x}$ and $w_{y}:=Gy+v_{y}$ , where $v_{x}\in Tx$ and $v_{y}\in Ty$ , respectively.
Then, from Young’s inequality, we have $(1+\tau)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}-\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\geq-\frac{(1+\tau)}{\tau}\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}$ for any $\tau>0$ .
Utilizing the assumptions in [100, 102], Jensen’s inequality, and the last relation, we can easily prove that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w_{x}-w_{y},x-y\rangle&=&\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle+\langle v_{x}-v_{y},x-y\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{1}{L}\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big]-\rho\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho\big]\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big]+\rho\big[(1+\tau)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}-\|v_{x}-v_{y}\|^{2}\big]\\
&\geq&\big[\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho\big]\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big]-\frac{(1+\tau)\rho}{\tau}\|w_{x}-w_{y}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
This inequality shows that $\Phi:=G+T$ studied in [100, 102] satisfies (6) in Assumption 1 with $\rho_{n}:=\frac{(1+\tau)\rho}{\tau}\geq 0$ , $0\leq\rho_{c}\leq\frac{1}{L}-(1+\tau)\rho$ .
Clearly, to guarantee $\rho_{c}\geq 0$ , we require $0\leq L\rho\leq\frac{1}{1+\tau}<1$ .
Example 4.
Since $R(x,y)\leq M$ , we have $n\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq M\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(G_{i}x-G_{i}y)\|^{2}$ .
It implies that $\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq\frac{M}{n^{2}}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(G_{i}x-G_{i}y)\|^{2}=M\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}$ .
Now, since $G$ is $\rho$ -co-hypomonotone, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle&\geq&-\rho\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+(\rho_{n}-\rho)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+\frac{\rho_{n}-\rho}{M}\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big].\end{array}$
Note that in order for the last inequality to hold (and to match the condition (7) in Assumption 1), we require $\rho_{n}>\rho$ .
In this case, we get $\rho_{c}:=\frac{\rho_{n}-\rho}{M}$ .
Example 5.
By the definitions of $\bar{\sigma}_{i}$ and $\underline{\sigma}$ , we have $\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|\leq\bar{\sigma}_{i}\|x-y\|$ and $\|Gx-Gy\|\geq\underline{\sigma}\|x-y\|$ , respectively.
Combining these two relations, we get $\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|\leq\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{i}}{\underline{\sigma}}\|Gx-Gy\|$ .
This implies $\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big]:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}y\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{\sigma}_{i}^{2}\right)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}$ , where we redefine $\bar{\sigma}^{2}:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{\sigma}_{i}^{2}$ .
Now, since $G$ is $\rho$ -co-hypomonotone, we have
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle Gx-Gy,x-y\rangle&\geq&-\rho\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}=-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+(\rho_{n}-\rho)\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-\rho_{n}\|Gx-Gy\|^{2}+\frac{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}(\rho_{n}-\rho)\mathbb{E}_{\xi}\big[\|\mathbf{G}(x,\xi)-\mathbf{G}(y,\xi)\|^{2}\big].\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Note that in order for the last inequality to hold (and to match the condition (7) in Assumption 1), we require $\rho_{n}>\rho$ .
In this case, we also get $\rho_{c}:=\frac{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}(\rho_{n}-\rho)$ .
Appendix B The Proof of Lemmas 5.1, 5.2, and 5.4.
This appendix presents the full proof of the three lemmas: Lemmas 5.1, 5.2, and 5.4.
B.1 The proof of Lemma 5.1.
First, using $e^{k}$ and $\hat{e}^{k}$ from (45), we can write $\widetilde{G}y^{k}=Gy^{k}+e^{k}$ and $\widetilde{G}y^{k-1}=Gx^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}$ .
Denote $w^{k}:=Gx^{k}+v^{k}$ and $\hat{w}^{k}:=Gy^{k-1}+v^{k}$ for a given $v^{k}\in Tx^{k}$ .
Then, we can rewrite the first and third lines of (VAPEG) equivalently to
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}x^{k+1}&=&sz^{k}+(t_{k}-s)x^{k}-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}$
Rearranging this expression in two different ways, and then using $sz^{k}=sz^{k+1}+\gamma_{k}(\widetilde{G}y^{k-1}+v^{k})=sz^{k+1}+\gamma_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})$ from the last line of (VAPEG), we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(x^{k+1}-x^{k})&=&s(t_{k}-s)(z^{k}-x^{k})-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}),\\
t_{k}(t_{k}-s)(x^{k+1}-x^{k})&=&st_{k}(z^{k}-x^{k+1})-\eta t_{k}^{2}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\beta_{k}t_{k}^{2}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}),\\
&=&st_{k}(z^{k+1}-x^{k+1})-\eta t_{k}^{2}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma_{k})(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}$
Second, by the condition (6) of $\Phi$ in Assumption 1 and using the notion $|\!\|\cdot|\!\|$ , we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle&\geq&-\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Combining the last three expressions, we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma_{k})\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\eta t_{k}\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\beta_{k}t_{k}\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle-\gamma_{k}t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}$
Now, by Young’s inequality, for any $c_{1}>0$ and $c_{2}>0$ , we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[2]}&:=&\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\frac{c_{1}}{2t_{k}}\|t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{c_{1}t_{k}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{c_{1}(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{2t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+c_{1}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\frac{t_{k}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2},\\
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&:=&-\langle t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\frac{c_{2}\eta}{2\beta_{k}t_{k}}\|t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-s)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{c_{2}\eta t_{k}}{2\beta_{k}}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{2\beta_{k}t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{\beta_{k}}\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2},\\
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&-\langle w^{k+1},\hat{e}^{k}\rangle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Substituting $\mathcal{T}_{[2]}$ , $\mathcal{T}_{[3]}$ , and $\mathcal{T}_{[4]}$ into $\mathcal{T}_{[1]}$ , we can further derive that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2(\rho_{n}-\beta_{k})+\frac{c_{1}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)+c_{1}\eta t_{k}+c_{2}\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\big[2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)+c_{1}\eta(t_{k}-s)+c_{2}\eta(t_{k}-s)-\beta_{k}t_{k}-\gamma_{k}\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{c_{2}\eta}+\gamma_{k}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Next, assume that $(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)-t_{k}\beta_{k}-\gamma_{k}=0$ .
This condition is guaranteed if we update $\beta_{k}$ as in (46), i.e.:
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\beta_{k}=\frac{(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)-\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}.\end{array}$
Partly substituting this $\beta_{k}$ into $\mathcal{T}_{[1]}$ and using the following identity
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle&=&\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\end{array}$
we can simplify the last expression $\mathcal{T}_{[1]}$ as
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta+\frac{(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}-\frac{2(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}+2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2c_{1}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{c_{2}\eta}+\gamma_{k}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}.\end{array}$
For a given $\omega\geq 0$ , we define $M^{2}:=2(1+\omega)L^{2}$ .
Then, from the second and third lines of (VAPEG), we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}+e^{k})+\eta t_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})=-\eta t_{k}(\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k})-\eta t_{k}(e^{k}-\hat{e}^{k}).\end{array}$
Applying the condition (3) from Assumption 1, we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+E_{k}=\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}\|^{2}+E_{k}\leq(1+\omega)L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}$
where
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}E_{k}&:=&(\alpha-1)\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}\|^{2}+(1-\alpha)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&(1-\alpha)\big[|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Combining the last two expressions, we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}E_{k}&\leq&(1+\omega)L^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&(1+\omega)L^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}+e^{k}-\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}-\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}$
for some $\theta>0$ .
Multiplying both sides of this inequality by $\frac{\eta}{2}$ and partly expanding the resulting expression and rearranging the result, we can deduce that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}\\
&&-{~}\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Adding this inequality to $\mathcal{T}_{[1]}$ yields
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&s(t_{k}-s)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\frac{\eta t_{k}[t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}]}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta+\frac{(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}-\frac{2(c_{1}\eta+c_{2}\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{t_{k}}{2}\Big(\eta t_{k}-\big[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta t_{k}+\gamma_{k}+2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\Big)\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{\eta}{2c_{1}}+\frac{(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{3}}{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{2}+\frac{(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}}{2}\big)t_{k}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Next, utilizing $s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})=-\gamma_{k}(w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k})$ from the last line of (VAPEG) and Young’s inequality, for any $c_{k}>0$ , we can derive that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}&:=&\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{\gamma_{k}}\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&s(s-1)\langle w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{(s-1)\gamma_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&s(s-1)\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)c_{k}}{2}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(s-1)\gamma_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{s-1}{2}\big(2\gamma_{k}+\frac{1}{c_{k}}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Since $\gamma_{k}:=\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}$ and $t_{k}=t_{k-1}+1$ from (46) for some $\gamma\geq 0$ , we can easily prove that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k-1}}-\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k}}&=&\frac{1}{2\gamma}\big(\frac{t_{k-1}}{t_{k-1}-1}-\frac{t_{k}}{t_{k}-1}\big)=\frac{(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-1)-t_{k}(t_{k}-2)}{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}=\frac{1}{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Therefore, if we choose $c_{k}:=\frac{1}{\gamma_{k-1}}-\frac{1}{\gamma_{k}}=\frac{1}{\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}$ , then we have $\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k}}+\frac{c_{k}}{2}=\frac{1}{2\gamma_{k-1}}$ .
Using this relation, the expression $\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}$ implies that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big]\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.}\end{array}$
Since $t_{k}-t_{k-1}=1$ due to (46), we have $s(t_{k-1}-t_{k}+s)=s(s-1)$ .
Adding $\mathcal{T}_{[4]}$ to $\mathcal{T}_{[1]}$ and using $s(t_{k-1}-t_{k}+s)=s(s-1)$ , we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k})}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta t_{k}-\gamma_{k}-2\rho_{n}(t_{k}-s)\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Big\{\frac{t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big[2\rho_{n}+\eta-\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+4\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}^{2}}\big]+\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}\Big\}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Big[\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+\frac{(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}}{2}\big)t_{k}+\gamma(s-1)\big(\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t_{k}-1}}{t_{k}}+\frac{(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}{2}\big)\Big]\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{\eta}{2c_{1}}+\frac{(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{3}}{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Now, we recall from (49) that
${\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k+1}&:=&(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta t_{k}^{2}-2\rho_{n}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)-\gamma(t_{k}-1),\\
\hat{a}_{k}&:=&\big[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}\big](t_{k}-s)^{2}+s(\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}},\\
b_{k}&:=&\big(\frac{1}{2c_{1}}+\frac{(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{2}}{2}\big)\eta t_{k}^{2},\\
\hat{b}_{k}&:=&\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+\frac{(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}}{2}\big)t_{k}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big].\end{array}\right.}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Then, we can rewrite the last expression $\mathcal{T}_{[5]}$ as
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[6]}&:=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{a_{k+1}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-st_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-z^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k}}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}E_{k}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s-M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k})}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-b_{k}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\hat{b}_{k}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Substituting $\mathcal{P}_{k}$ from (47) into this new expression $\mathcal{T}_{[6]}$ , we eventually get (48).
$\square$
B.2 The proof of Lemma 5.2.
First, since $s>2$ due to (50) and $t_{k}=t_{k-1}+1$ due to (46), we have $t_{k}(t_{k}-s)\leq(t_{k}-1)^{2}$ .
Thus, for $\psi:=(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}$ , from (49), we can show that
${\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&:=&\psi t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)=\psi(t_{k}-1)^{2}+(2s\rho_{n}-\gamma)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma,\\
\hat{a}_{k}&:=&\psi(t_{k}-s)^{2}+s(\eta+2\rho_{n})(t_{k}-s)+\frac{2\gamma(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}}\\
&\leq&\psi(t_{k}-s)^{2}+[s(\eta+2\rho_{n})+2\gamma](t_{k}-1)-s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n}).\end{array}\right.}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Using these relations, we can lower bound
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}&\geq&\psi(s-1)(2t_{k}-s-1)-(s\eta+3\gamma)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&=&[2\psi(s-1)-s\eta-3\gamma](t_{k}-1)-\psi(s-1)^{2}+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&=&\big[[s-2-2(c_{1}+c_{2})(s-1)]\eta-4\rho_{n}(s-1)-3\gamma\big](t_{k}-1)+\gamma+s(s-1)(\eta+2\rho_{n})\\
&&-{~}(s-1)^{2}[(1-c_{1}-c_{2})\eta-2\rho_{n}].\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Next, let us choose $c_{1}:=\frac{s-2}{16(s-1)}>0$ and $c_{2}:=\frac{s-2}{8(s-1)}>0$ .
Then, we obtain from the above expressions that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&=&\big[\frac{(13s-10)\eta}{16(s-1)}-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1),\\
a_{k}-\hat{a}_{k}&\geq&\big[\frac{5(s-2)}{8}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}-3\gamma\big](t_{k}-1)+\frac{(s-1)(3s+10)\eta}{16}+2(2s^{2}-3s+1)\rho_{n}+\gamma=:\phi_{k}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Using $c_{1}+c_{2}=\frac{3(s-2)}{16(s-1)}$ and $(s-2)\eta\geq 8(s-1)\rho_{n}$ from (50), we can derive from (46) that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\beta_{k}&=&\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+2\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)-\gamma_{k}}{t_{k}}\leq\frac{[(c_{1}+c_{2})\eta+2\rho_{n}](t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}=\big[\frac{3(s-2)}{16(s-1)}+\frac{2\rho_{n}}{\eta}]\frac{\eta(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}\leq\frac{7(s-2)\eta(t_{k}-s)}{16(s-1)t_{k}}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Next, let us choose $\theta:=8$ .
Then,
since $M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq\frac{t_{k}-s}{t_{k}}\ \leq 1$ and $s>2$ from (50), and $\beta_{k}\leq\frac{7(s-2)\eta(t_{k}-s)}{16(s-1)t_{k}}$ , we can upper bound $b_{k}$ and $\hat{b}_{k}$ from (49) respectively as follows:
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}b_{k}&=&\big(\frac{1}{2c_{1}}+\frac{(1+\theta)M^{2}\eta^{2}}{2}\big)\eta t_{k}^{2}\leq\big[\frac{8(s-1)}{s-2}+\frac{9}{2}\big]\eta t_{k}^{2}=\frac{(25s-34)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2(s-2)},\\
\hat{b}_{k}&=&\big(\frac{\beta_{k}^{2}t_{k}}{2c_{2}\eta}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2t_{k}}+\frac{(1+\theta^{-1})M^{2}\eta^{3}t_{k}}{2}\big)t_{k}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big[\frac{2(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)\big]\\
&\leq&\frac{49(s-2)\eta(t_{k}-s)^{2}}{64(s-1)}+\frac{9\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{16}+\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{2}+\frac{\gamma(s-1)(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma(t_{k}-1)(t_{k}-2)}{2}\\
&\leq&\frac{49(s-2)\eta}{64(s-1)}[t_{k-1}^{2}-2(s-1)t_{k-1}+(s-1)^{2}]+\frac{9\eta}{16}[t_{k-1}^{2}-(s-2)t_{k-1}-(s-1)]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}(t_{k-1}^{2}-t_{k-1})+\frac{\gamma t_{k-1}}{2}+(s-1)\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{49(s-2)\eta}{64(s-1)}+\frac{9\eta}{16}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}-\big[\frac{49(s-2)\eta}{32}+\frac{9(s-2)\eta}{16}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\eta(s-1)\big[\frac{49(s-2)}{64}-\frac{9}{16}\big]+(s-1)\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{(85s-134)\eta}{64(s-1)}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}-\big[\frac{67(s-2)\eta}{32}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}+\frac{(s-1)[(49s-134)\eta+64\gamma]}{64}\\
&\leq&\big[\frac{(85s-134)\eta}{64(s-1)}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}^{2},\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
provided that $\big[\frac{67(s-2)\eta}{32}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\geq\frac{(s-1)[(49s-134)\eta+64\gamma]}{64}$ .
Using these bounds into (48), we get
$\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\big[(1-\alpha)\big(|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big)+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{(25s-34)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2(s-2)}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\big[\frac{85s-134}{64(s-1)}+\frac{(s-1)\gamma}{2\eta}\big]\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt$
By Young’s inequality, from (45) we get $\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\big(1+\frac{1}{8}\big)\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}+\big(1+8\big)\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}=\frac{9}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+9\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}$ .
Utilizing this relation into the last estimate, we obtain (51).
Finally, since $t_{k-1}\geq s$ , $s\geq 2$ , and $0<\gamma\leq\frac{9}{16}\eta$ from (50), we can prove that the condition $\big[\frac{67(s-2)\eta}{32}+\frac{(s-2)\gamma}{2}\big]t_{k-1}\geq\frac{(s-1)[(49s-134)\eta+64\gamma]}{64}$ automatically holds.
$\square$
B.3 The proof of Lemma 5.4.
Since $w^{k}:=Gx^{k}+v^{k}$ for $v^{k}\in Tx^{k}$ , from (47), for any $c_{k}>0$ , we can rewrite $\mathcal{P}_{k}$ as
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}&=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)t_{k-1}}{2\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&\frac{a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2c_{k}^{2}}\|c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}w^{k}-s(z^{k}-x^{\star})\|^{2}+\frac{s^{2}}{2}\big[\frac{(s-1)t_{k-1}}{\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)}-\frac{1}{c_{k}^{2}}\big]\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}$
Let us choose $c_{k}^{2}:=\frac{\eta}{4}$ .
Then, we have $a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}=\big[\frac{3(3s-2)}{16(s-1)}\eta-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+2s\rho_{n}t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)$ .
Using this expression, $\frac{t_{k-1}}{t_{k-1}-1}\geq 1$ , and noting that $\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho_{*}\|w^{k}\|^{2}$ from (5) of Assumption 1, the last expression leads to
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}&\geq&\frac{A_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{s^{2}}{2}\big(\frac{s-1}{\gamma}-\frac{4}{\eta}\big)\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\end{array}$
which proves (53), where $A_{k}:=a_{k}-c_{k}^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}-2s\rho_{*}t_{k-1}=\big[\frac{3(3s-2)}{16(s-1)}\eta-2\rho_{n}\big]t_{k-1}^{2}+2s(\rho_{n}-\rho_{*})t_{k-1}-\gamma(t_{k-1}-1)$ .
$\square$
Appendix C The Missing Proofs of Lemmas in Section 5.
This appendix presents the missing proofs of the technical lemmas in Section 5.
C.1 The proof of Lemma 5.5.
First, let us choose $\gamma:=\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\eta$ in (46) of Lemma 5.1, which satisfies the condition $0<\gamma\leq\frac{9}{16}\eta$ in (50).
This leads to the update of $\gamma_{k}$ as in (19).
In addition, $\varphi$ in (52) of Lemma 5.2 becomes $\varphi:=\frac{9(85s-134)}{64(s-1)}+\frac{9(s-2)}{64}$ .
Next, under the conditions of Theorem 3.1 and the choice
of $\gamma$ as above, all the conditions of Lemmas 5.2 and 5.4 are fulfilled.
Now, since $\rho_{c}=0$ and $\alpha=1$ , taking the conditional expectation $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\cdot\big]$ on both sides of (51) in Lemma 5.2, we obtain from this inequality that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{\varphi\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(25s-34)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2(s-2)}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\varphi\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Rearranging the last two terms and using $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]$ from the first line of (15), we have
$\hskip-12.91663pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{\varphi\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}-\left[\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}+\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]\\
&&+{~}\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(s-2)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt$
(95)
Now, multiplying the second line of (15) by $\Lambda_{0}\eta t_{k}^{2}$ for $\Lambda_{0}$ given in (18), then rearranging the results, we get
$\begin{array}[]{llcl}&0&\geq&\Lambda_{0}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)t_{k}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\Lambda_{0}\Theta L^{2}\eta t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}$
Since $t_{k}=k+s+1$ , we can show that $t_{k-1}^{2}\leq t_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}$ and $\frac{(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}-t_{k}^{2}\geq\frac{(2s+1)t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)}{s^{2}}$ .
Using these relations, we obtain from the last inequality that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\frac{\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\Theta L^{2}(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&{~}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Lambda_{0}[s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}]}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}$
From (3) of Assumption 1 with $\alpha=1$ , we also have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}=\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Combining the last two expressions, we can derive from (95) that
$\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\Big[\frac{\Lambda_{0}[s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}]}{s^{2}}-\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}-\varphi-\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\Big]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{(s-2)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big(\frac{\varphi}{8}+\frac{\Lambda_{0}\Theta(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)\Delta_{k-1}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt$
(96)
Next, since $\frac{2s+1}{(s+1)^{2}}<\kappa\leq 1$ from Theorem 3.1, we have $\Lambda_{0}:=\frac{s^{2}}{s^{2}-(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}}\left[\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}+\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]>0$ .
In addition, let us denote $\Lambda_{1}:=\frac{\varphi}{4}+\frac{2\Lambda_{0}\Theta(s+1)^{2}}{s^{2}}$ and choose $\omega:=\Lambda_{1}+\frac{s-2}{16(s-1)}$ as in (18).
Then, using this $\Lambda_{0}$ and $\Lambda_{1}$ , the relation $\omega=\Lambda_{1}+\frac{s-2}{16(s-1)}$ , and $\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho_{*}\|w^{k}\|^{2}$ from Assumption 1, we can show from (96) that
${\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\big[\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}-s(s-1)\rho_{*}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(s-2)\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)\eta(s+1)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\frac{\Lambda_{0}\eta(1-\kappa)(s+1)^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{s^{2}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\left[\varphi+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\right]\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2s+1)\Lambda_{0}(1-\kappa)}}{s^{2}}\eta t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-s)\Delta_{k-1}+\frac{(s-2)\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{0}\eta\delta_{k}.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt}\vskip-2.15277pt$
(97)
Now, since $8(s-1)\rho_{n}\leq(s-2)\eta$ and $0\leq\rho_{*}\leq\rho_{n}$ , for $k\geq 0$ , $s>2$ , and $\gamma:=\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\eta$ , we have
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\phi_{k}-2\rho_{*}s(s-1)&=&\big[\frac{5(s-2)}{8}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}-3\gamma\big](t_{k}-1)+\frac{(s-1)(3s+10)\eta}{16}\\
&&+{~}2(2s-1)(s-1)\rho_{n}+\gamma-2\rho_{*}s(s-1)\\
&\geq&\frac{(s-2)(4s-7)\eta}{32(s-1)}(k+1)+\frac{(s-2)(4s-7)\eta}{32}+\frac{(s-1)(3s+10)\eta}{16}\\
&&+{~}2(2s-1)(s-1)\rho_{n}+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\eta-2\rho_{n}s(s-1)\\
&=&\frac{(s-2)(4s-7)\eta}{32(s-1)}(k+1)+\frac{10s^{3}-11s^{2}-4s+4}{32(s-1)}\eta+2(s-1)^{2}\rho_{n}\\
&\geq&\frac{(s-2)(4s-7)\eta}{32(s-1)}(k+1)>0.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Furthermore, since $t_{k}\geq s+1$ , to guarantee $\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\leq 1$ , we impose $(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1$ .
However, since $\omega$ is given in (18), the condition $(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1$ is guaranteed if we choose $\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}$ .
In addition, since $\eta\geq\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{s-2}$ , we finally get
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{s-2}\leq\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}=\frac{\lambda}{L},\quad\textrm{where}\quad\lambda:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(1+\omega)(s+1)}}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
This inequality holds if we choose $\eta$ as in (19).
Finally, if we recall $\mathcal{L}_{k}$ from (54), then using the last relations and $\epsilon_{1}:=1-(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\geq 0$ , (97) leads to (55).
$\square$
C.2 The proof of Lemma 5.6.
First, from the first and second lines of (VAPEG), we can easily derive that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}u^{k}&:=&s(z^{k}-x^{k})=t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+t_{k}(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1}).\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
(98)
Using this expression, the third line of (VAPEG), and $x^{k+1}-y^{k}=-\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1})$ , we can derive
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}u^{k+1}-\frac{(t_{k}-s)}{t_{k}}u^{k}&=&u^{k+1}-u^{k}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}u^{k}\\
&=&s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-s(x^{k+1}-x^{k})+s(y^{k}-x^{k})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&s(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-s(x^{k+1}-y^{k})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&-\gamma_{k}(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})+s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1})+s(\eta-\beta_{k})(\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k-1})\\
&=&s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k})+[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]\hat{w}^{k}+s\eta e^{k}-(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})e^{k-1}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Denote $d^{k}:=\frac{t_{k}}{s}\big[s\eta(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k})+[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]\hat{w}^{k}+s\eta e^{k}-(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})e^{k-1}\big]$ .
Then, the last estimate is rewritten as $u^{k+1}=\big(1-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\big)u^{k}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}d^{k}$ .
By convexity of $\|\cdot\|^{2}$ , $\frac{s}{t_{k}}\in(0,1]$ , and Young’s inequality, we have
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|u^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{t_{k}-s}{t_{k}}\|u^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{s}{t_{k}}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|u^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\|u^{k}\|^{2}+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4[s(\eta-\beta_{k})-\gamma_{k}]^{2}t_{k}}{s}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4(s\beta_{k}+\gamma_{k})^{2}t_{k}}{s}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Note that from Lemmas 5.2 and 5.5, we have $\beta_{k}\leq\frac{7(s-2)\eta(t_{k}-s)}{16(s-1)t_{k}}\leq\eta$ and $\gamma_{k}=\frac{(s-2)\eta(t_{k}-1)}{32(s-1)t_{k}}\leq\eta$ .
Using these inequalities into the last estimate and taking the full expectation on both sides of the resulting inequality, we get
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathbb{E}\big[\|u^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]&\leq&\mathbb{E}\big[\|u^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{s}{t_{k}}\mathbb{E}\big[\|u^{k}\|^{2}\big]+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{4(2s+1)^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{s}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]+4s\eta^{2}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{4(s+1)^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{s}\mathbb{E}\big[\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\big].\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
(99)
Applying Lemma A.1 to the last inequality by using the summability bounds in (56), we get
${\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|u^{k}\|^{2}\big]=\lim_{k\to\infty}s^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0,\ \ \textrm{and}\ \ \sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\tfrac{s}{t_{k}}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty.}\vskip-2.15277pt$
These relations imply (57).
Next, since $t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta t_{k}\big(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}+e^{k}-e^{k-1}\big)$ by (VAPEG), by Young’s inequality, we can show that
${\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}}\vskip-2.15277pt$
Combining this inequality and (56), we can prove that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]&<&+\infty.\end{array}$
This is exactly the first summability bound of (58).
Finally, using again (98) and then applying Young’s inequality, we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{3s^{2}}{t_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+6\eta^{2}t_{k}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}+6\eta^{2}t_{k}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2},\\
t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Utilizing the first line of (58), (56), and the second summability bound of (57), we can also derive from these inequalities that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty.\end{array}$
These prove the last two lines of (58), respectively.
$\square$
C.3 The proof of Lemma 5.7.
Since $\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]\leq\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ , from (53) of Lemma 5.4, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{A_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{s^{2}[(s-1)\eta-4\gamma]}{2\gamma\eta}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]\leq\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{0},\end{array}$
where we have used $\frac{s^{2}[(s-1)\eta-4\gamma]}{2\gamma\eta}=\frac{2s^{2}(8s^{2}-17s+10)}{(s-2)\eta}>0$ for all $s>2$ .
This inequality implies that $\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]\leq M_{0}:=\frac{2\gamma\eta\bar{\mathcal{P}}_{0}}{s^{2}[(s-1)\eta-4\gamma]}$ .
Now, from Young’s inequality, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle&\leq&\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{s}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}^{2}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\\
-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{k}\rangle&\leq&\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}}{s}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\eta t_{k}}{s}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Using Young’s inequality in ①, the condition (5) from Assumption 1 in ②, and the last two relations, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|z^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}&=&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}+\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny\textcircled{1}}}{{\leq}}&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle\hat{e}^{k},z^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{2\gamma_{k}}{s}\langle w^{k},z^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\gamma_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{s}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma_{k}}{st_{k}^{2}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma_{k}^{2}}{s^{2}}\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny\textcircled{2}}}{{\leq}}&\big(1+\frac{\eta}{st_{k}^{2}}\big)\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{\eta t_{k}}{s}+\frac{2\eta\rho_{*}}{s}+\frac{2\eta^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta}{st_{k}}\|z^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{s}+\frac{2\eta^{2}}{s^{2}}\big)\|\hat{e}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
(100)
Taking the full expectation on both sides of this inequality and noting that the last three terms are summable, applying Lemma A.1, we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]$ exists.
However, since $\lim_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ by (57), using Minkowski’s inequality $\big|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]}-\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]}\big|\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]}$ , we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]$ also exists.
Since $\{t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\}$ is bounded, there exists $\bar{M}>0$ such that $t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\bar{M}^{2}$ .
Using this inequality and $\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ from (57), we can show that when $k\to\infty$ :
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k-1}^{2}|\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]|^{2}&\leq&t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\bar{M}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|x^{k}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\to 0.\end{array}$
This expression implies that $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]=0$ .
From (47), we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}=\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Since $\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k}\big]$ and $\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]$ exist, $\gamma_{k-1}\to\gamma>0$ as $k\to\infty$ , and $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle\big]=0$ , we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}a_{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]$ exists.
Since $\lim_{k\to\infty}\frac{t_{k-1}^{2}}{a_{k}}=\frac{1}{\psi}$ , we conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]$ exists.
However, since $\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]<+\infty$ by our assumption, we can easily argue that $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ , which proves the first limit of (59).
Note that $\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}=\|Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}$ by Assumption 1.
Utilizing this relation and the first line of (58), we can conclude that $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ .
Finally, by Young’s inequality, we have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k-1}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Since $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ and $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ , the last inequality implies that $\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k-1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\big]=0$ , which proves the second limit of (59).
$\square$
C.4 The proof of Lemma 5.8.
First, if we choose $\gamma:=\frac{(s-2)\eta}{32(s-1)}$ , then $\varphi$ in (52) becomes $\varphi=\varphi_{s}:=\frac{9(85s-134)}{64(s-1)}+\frac{9(s-2)}{64}$ .
Second, applying (3) from Assumption 1 with $x=x^{k+1}$ and $y=y^{k}$ , we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tau\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&\geq&\tau\omega(1-\alpha)|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\tau\omega\alpha\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2},\quad\tau\in(0,1].\end{array}$
Using $|\!\|\cdot|\!\|\geq\|\cdot\|$ and this inequality, we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&(1-\alpha)\big(|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big)+\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\tau\omega(1-\alpha)|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\tau\omega\alpha\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+(1-\tau)\omega L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Next, substituting this relation into (51) of Lemma 5.2, we can derive that
$\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\tau)\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{(25s-34)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2(s-2)}\|e^{k}\|^{2}-\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt$
(101)
Since $w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}=Gx^{k+1}-Gy^{k}$ and $w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}=Gx^{k}-Gy^{k-1}$ , by Young’s inequality, the relation $|\!\|\cdot|\!\|\geq\|\cdot\|$ , and (3) from Assumption 1, we can derive that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\geq\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2},\\
|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2},\\
L^{2}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\geq\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
(102)
Then, since $t_{k}^{2}\geq t_{k}(t_{k}-s)$ and $t_{k-1}^{2}\geq t_{k}(t_{k}-s)$ for $s\geq 2$ , using the last two inequalities and the conditions
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta\geq 4\rho_{c}\quad\textrm{and}\quad(1-\tau)\omega\eta\geq 2\big[\frac{\varphi_{s}}{8}+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\big]\eta,\end{array}$
(103)
we can prove that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&\frac{\tau\omega(1-\alpha)\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\tau)\omega\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\overset{\tiny\eqref{eq:para_cond10_proof}}{\geq}&\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|Gx^{k+1}-Gx^{k}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[\frac{\varphi_{s}}{8}+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\big]\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\overset{\tiny\eqref{eq:three_estimates_proof1}}{\geq}&\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}-\frac{2\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{8}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(s-2)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Substituting this expression into (101) and taking the conditional expectation $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\cdot\big]$ on both sides of the result, and splitting $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]$ and $\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}$ into two parts, we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{8}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}\big]-\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}+\frac{(s-2)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}+\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big].\end{array}\hskip-21.52771pt$
(104)
Now, for a given $\bar{c}\in(0,1)$ , we can derive from (27) of Definition 4.1 that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]&\leq&\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{1-\bar{c}}{\bar{c}}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}~+~\frac{\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}}}.\end{array}$
(105)
Suppose that there exists $\epsilon_{2}\geq 0$ such that $\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}t_{k-1}^{2}}\leq\frac{1-\bar{c}}{\bar{c}}-\epsilon_{2}$ for $\bar{c}\in(0,1)$ , which holds if $\bar{c}\leq\frac{1}{1+\epsilon_{2}}\big[1-\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{t_{k-1}^{2}}\big]$ .
Moreover, to handle the term $|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}$ , we also need to impose $\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\geq 0$ , leading to $\bar{c}\geq\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{3\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}}{\rho_{c}(t_{k}-s)}$ .
Combining both conditions on $\bar{c}$ , we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{3\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}}{\rho_{c}(t_{k}-s)}\leq\bar{c}\leq\frac{1}{1+\epsilon_{2}}\big[1-\frac{(1-\kappa_{k})t_{k}^{2}}{t_{k-1}^{2}}\big].\end{array}$
This is exactly the condition (76).
In this case, using $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]$ from (27) of Definition 4.1, (105) becomes
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]&\leq&\frac{(1-\bar{c})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}-\frac{(1-\bar{c})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]+\frac{\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}}+\frac{\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}|\!\|Gy^{k}-Gy^{k-1}|\!\|^{2}-\eta\epsilon_{2}t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}$
Multiplying both sides of this inequality by $\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]$ , then substituting the result into (104)
and using the condition $\frac{\rho_{c}t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{3}-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{\eta\Theta_{k}t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\geq 0$ , we obtain
$\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{8}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\rho_{c}t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[|\!\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}|\!\|^{2}\big]-\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]-\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{(1-\bar{c})\eta t_{k}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\Delta_{k}\big]-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{(1-\bar{c})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(s-2)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\epsilon_{2}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}}.\end{array}\hskip-4.30554pt$
Recall the Lyapunov function $\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}$ from (77) as follows:
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}&:=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+st_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-z^{k}\rangle+\frac{s^{2}(s-1)}{2\gamma_{k-1}}\|z^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\rho_{c}t_{k-1}^{2}|\!\|w^{k}-\hat{w}^{k}|\!\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\varphi_{s}\eta L^{2}t_{k-1}^{2}}{8}\|x^{k}-y^{k-1}\|^{2}+\varphi_{s}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\|e^{k-1}\|^{2}+\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{(1-\bar{c})\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{\bar{c}}\Delta_{k-1}.\end{array}$
Using this function, the last expression becomes
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}-\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}\big]&\geq&\frac{\eta st_{k}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\phi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+s(s-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-s)}{2}\big(1-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\big)\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\frac{\tau\omega\alpha\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\frac{(s-2)\eta L^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{32(s-1)}\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\big]+\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\epsilon_{2}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}\Delta_{k-1}-\big[\varphi_{s}+\frac{25s-34}{2(s-2)}\big]\frac{\eta\delta_{k}}{\bar{c}}.\end{array}$
This exactly proves (78), with $\tau$ chosen as follows.
If we choose $\tau:=\frac{4\rho_{c}}{(1-\alpha)\omega\eta}\in(0,1)$ , then both conditions in (103) hold, provided that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\omega\geq\frac{4\rho_{c}}{(1-\alpha)\eta}+2\big[\frac{\varphi_{s}}{8}+\frac{s-2}{32(s-1)}\big].\end{array}$
(106)
Since $\rho_{c}\leq\rho_{n}$ from Assumption 1 and $(s-2)\eta\geq 8(s-1)\rho_{n}$ from (50), the condition (106) holds if we choose $\omega=\hat{\omega}:=\frac{s-2}{2(1-\alpha)(s-1)}+\frac{\varphi_{s}}{4}+\frac{s-2}{16(s-1)}$ as in (30).
To guarantee the nonnegativity of the coefficients on the right-hand side of (78), we need to impose $\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}}{t_{k}-s}\leq 1$ .
Since $t_{k}\geq s+1$ , the last condition holds if $(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1$ .
However, since $\hat{\omega}$ is given in (30), the condition $(s+1)M^{2}\eta^{2}\leq 1$ is guaranteed if we choose $\eta\leq\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(s+1)(1+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hat{\omega}})}}$ .
Since $\eta\geq\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{s-2}$ , we finally get $\frac{8(s-1)\rho_{n}}{s-2}\leq\eta\leq\frac{\hat{\lambda}}{L}$ as in (75) with $\hat{\lambda}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(s+1)(1+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\hat{\omega}})}}$ .
Moreover, utilizing (46) and Lemma 5.2 with $c_{1}=\frac{s-2}{16(s-1)}$ , $c_{2}=\frac{s-2}{8(s-1)}$ , $t_{k}=k+s+1$ , and $\gamma=\frac{(s-2)\eta}{32(s-1)}$ as before, we also have $\gamma_{k}:=\frac{\gamma(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}=\frac{(s-2)\eta(k+s)}{32(s-1)(k+s+1)}$ and $\beta_{k}=\big[\frac{3(s-2)\eta}{16(s-1)}+2\rho_{n}]\frac{k+1}{k+s+1}-\frac{\gamma_{k}}{k+s+1}$ as in (75).
Since $\gamma=\frac{(s-2)\eta}{32(s-1)}$ , we also have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\phi_{k}&=&\big[\frac{5(s-2)}{8}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}-3\gamma\big](k+s)+\frac{(s-1)(3s+10)\eta}{16}+2(2s^{2}-3s+1)\rho_{n}+\gamma\\
&=&\big[\frac{(20s-23)(s-2)}{32(s-1)}\eta-4(s-1)\rho_{n}\big](k+s)+(s-1)\big[\frac{(3s+10)\eta}{16}+2(2s-1)\rho_{n}\big]+\frac{(s-2)\eta}{32(s-1)}\end{array}$
as desired.
$\square$
C.5 The proof of Corollary 3.4: Oracle complexity of VAPEG using (16).
Assume that $\widetilde{G}y^{k}$ satisfies the condition (15) with $\kappa=1$ , $\Theta=0$ , and $\delta_{k}:=\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{1+\nu}}$ for a given $\delta>0$ and $\nu>0$ .
Then, we have $\mathcal{S}_{\infty}:=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k}=\delta\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(k+s+1)^{1+\nu}}<+\infty$ .
Moreover, by (15) we also have $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\delta_{k}}{t_{k}^{2}}=\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}$ .
However, since $\widetilde{G}y^{k}$ is constructed by (16), we have $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b_{k}}$ , where $\sigma^{2}$ is the variance bound in Assumption 1.
Therefore, the condition $\mathbb{E}_{k}\big[\|e^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}$ holds if we impose $\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b_{k}}\leq\frac{\delta}{t_{k}^{3+\nu}}$ .
Hence, we can choose $b_{k}:=\big\lceil\frac{\sigma^{2}t_{k}^{3+\nu}}{\delta}\big\rceil=\big\lceil\frac{\sigma^{2}(k+s+1)^{3+\nu}}{\delta}\big\rceil$ as stated.
In this case, the expected total number of oracle calls $\mathbf{G}$ is at most
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]&:=&\sum_{k=0}^{K}b_{k}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta}\sum_{k=0}^{K}(k+s+1)^{3+\nu}=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{\sigma^{2}K^{4+\nu}}{\delta}\big).\end{array}$
By (22) of Theorem 3.1, the number of iterations $K$ to reach an $\epsilon$ -solution in expectation is $K=\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{-1}\right)$ .
Therefore, we obtain $\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta\epsilon^{4+\nu}}\big)$ as stated.
The expected total number of $J_{\eta T}$ evaluations is the same as $K$ , which is $\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{-1}\right)$ .
Alternatively, if we choose $b_{k}:=\big\lceil\frac{\sigma^{2}(k+s+1)^{3}\log(k+s+1)}{\delta}\big\rceil$ , then $\mathcal{S}_{K}:=\delta\sum_{k=0}^{K}\frac{1}{(k+s+1)\log(k+s+1)}\leq 1+\log(\log(K+s+1))$ .
Hence, from (22) of Theorem 3.1, to achieve $\mathbb{E}\big[\|Gx^{k}+v^{k}\|^{2}\big]\leq\epsilon^{2}$ , we require $\frac{C_{0}(\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}+\Lambda_{0}S_{K})}{(K+s+1)^{2}}\leq\epsilon^{2}$ .
Using the upper bound of $\mathcal{S}_{K}$ , we can show that $K=\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{1}{\epsilon\sqrt{\log(\epsilon^{-1})}}\big)$ .
Using this estimation of $K$ , we can estimate that $\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{T}_{K}\big]=\sum_{k=0}^{K}b_{k}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\delta}\sum_{k=0}^{K}(k+s+1)^{3}\log(k+s+1)\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}(K+s+1)^{4}\log(K+s+1)}{4\delta}\sim\mathcal{O}\big(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}\log(\epsilon^{-1})}\big)$ .
The expected total number of $J_{\eta T}$ evaluations is the same as $K$ , which is $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-1})$ .
$\square$
C.6 The proof of Proposition 6.1.
We divide the proof of Proposition 6.1 into several steps.
Step 1.
First, we show that $h$ defined in (88) is $\gamma$ -strongly convex and $L_{h}$ -smooth with $L_{h}:=\gamma+\nu\|C\|^{2}$ .
Indeed, $\nabla h(y)=\gamma y+\nu C^{\top}\tanh(Cy)$ and $\nabla^{2}h(y)=\gamma\mathbb{I}+\nu C^{\top}D(y)C$ , where $D(y):=\text{diag}\big(\operatorname{sech}^{2}(c_{1}^{\top}y),\cdots,\operatorname{sech}^{2}(c_{r}^{\top}y)\big)$ .
Since $0\preceq D(y)\preceq\mathbb{I}$ , we have
$\gamma\mathbb{I}\preceq\nabla^{2}h(y)\preceq\gamma\mathbb{I}+\nu C^{\top}C\preceq(\gamma+\nu\|C\|^{2})\mathbb{I}.$
Hence, $h$ is $\gamma$ -strongly convex and $L_{h}$ -smooth with $L_{h}:=\gamma+\nu\|C\|^{2}$ .
Step 2.
Next, we show that $\nabla{\tilde{\mathcal{R}}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)$ from (90) is $\frac{1}{\theta-1}$ -Lipschitz continuous.
The derivative of $\tilde{R}_{\lambda,\theta}$ is
$\tilde{R}^{\prime}_{\lambda,\theta}(t)=-\lambda\boldsymbol{1}_{\{t<-\theta\lambda\}}+\frac{t+\lambda}{\theta-1}\boldsymbol{1}_{\{-\theta\lambda\leq t<-\lambda\}}+\frac{t-\lambda}{\theta-1}\boldsymbol{1}_{\{\lambda\theta\lambda\}}$ .
It is continuous, monotone nondecreasing, and piece-wise affine, with slope either $0$ or $\frac{1}{\theta-1}$ .
Therefore, $\tilde{R}_{\lambda,\theta}$ is convex and continuously differentiable, and $\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)$ is $\frac{1}{\theta-1}$ -Lipschitz continuous.
Step 3.
We now prove (i).
Let $x=(z,y)$ and $x^{\prime}=(z^{\prime},y^{\prime})$ be arbitrary.
By Jensen’s inequality and the $L_{f}$ -average smoothness of $f$ , $f$ is $L_{f}$ -smooth.
Thus, by Steps 1–2, one can show that
$\begin{array}[]{llcl}&\|\nabla f(z)-\nabla f(z^{\prime})+B(y-y^{\prime})-\tau(\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)-\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\prime}))\|&\leq&\big(L_{f}+\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\big)\|z-z^{\prime}\|+\|B\|\|y-y^{\prime}\|,\\
&\|\nabla h(y)-\nabla h(y^{\prime})-B^{\top}(z-z^{\prime})\|&\leq&\|B\|\|z-z^{\prime}\|+L_{h}\|y-y^{\prime}\|.\end{array}$
Therefore, with $\tilde{L}_{f}:=L_{f}+\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}$ , we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\begin{bmatrix}\|[Gx-Gx^{\prime}]_{z}\|\\
\|[Gx-Gx^{\prime}]_{y}\|\end{bmatrix}\leq\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{L}_{f}&\|B\|\\
\|B\|&L_{h}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\|z-z^{\prime}\|\\
\|y-y^{\prime}\|\end{bmatrix}.\end{array}$
Hence,
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|Gx-Gx^{\prime}\|\leq\left\|\begin{bmatrix}\tilde{L}_{f}&\|B\|\\
\|B\|&L_{h}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}\|x-x^{\prime}\|.\end{array}$
The spectral norm of this symmetric matrix is its largest eigenvalue $L:=\frac{\tilde{L}_{f}+L_{h}+\sqrt{(\tilde{L}_{f}-L_{h})^{2}+4\|B\|^{2}}}{2}$ .
Step 4. Next, we prove (ii).
Let $x=(z,y)$ and $x^{\prime}=(z^{\prime},y^{\prime})$ , and take $w\in\Phi x$ and $w^{\prime}\in\Phi x^{\prime}$ .
By the definition of $T$ , there exist $\xi\in\tau\lambda\partial\|z\|_{1}+\mathcal{N}_{\mathcal{Z}}(z)$ and $\xi^{\prime}\in\tau\lambda\partial\|z^{\prime}\|_{1}+\mathcal{N}_{\mathcal{Z}}(z^{\prime})$ such that
$w=\begin{bmatrix}\nabla f(z)+By-\tau\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)+\xi\\
\nabla h(y)-B^{\top}z\end{bmatrix}\quad\text{and}\quad w^{\prime}=\begin{bmatrix}\nabla f(z^{\prime})+By^{\prime}-\tau\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\prime})+\xi^{\prime}\\
\nabla h(y^{\prime})-B^{\top}z^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}.$
Thus,
$w-w^{\prime}=\begin{bmatrix}\big(\nabla f(z)-\nabla f(z^{\prime})\big)+B(y-y^{\prime})-\tau\big(\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)-\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\prime})\big)+(\xi-\xi^{\prime})\\
\big(\nabla h(y)-\nabla h(y^{\prime}))-B^{\top}(z-z^{\prime})\end{bmatrix}.$
(107)
Taking its inner product with $x-x^{\prime}$ and using the convexity of $f$ , the monotonicity of $\tau\lambda\partial\|\cdot\|_{1}+\partial\delta_{\mathcal{Z}}$ , and Steps 1–2, we obtain
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w-w^{\prime},x-x^{\prime}\rangle&\geq&-\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\|z-z^{\prime}\|^{2}+\gamma\|y-y^{\prime}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
(108)
We consider two cases.
•
Case 1.
If $\langle w-w^{\prime},x-x^{\prime}\rangle\geq 0$ , then the desired co-hypomonotonicity inequality holds trivially.
•
Case 2.
If $\langle w-w^{\prime},x-x^{\prime}\rangle<0$ , then (108) implies $\|y-y^{\prime}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\tau}{\gamma(\theta-1)}\|z-z^{\prime}\|^{2}$ .
Hence, using $\sigma:=\sigma_{\min}(B^{\top})$ and the $L_{h}$ -smoothness of $h$ ,
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w-w^{\prime}\|&\geq&\|\big(\nabla h(y)-\nabla h(y^{\prime}))-B^{\top}(z-z^{\prime})\|\\
&\geq&\sigma\|z-z^{\prime}\|-L_{h}\|y-y^{\prime}\|\\
&\geq&\big(\sigma-L_{h}\big(\frac{\tau}{\gamma(\theta-1)}\big)^{1/2}\big)\|z-z^{\prime}\|>0.\end{array}$
Combining this estimate with (108) gives
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w-w^{\prime},x-x^{\prime}\rangle&\geq&-\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\|z-z^{\prime}\|^{2}\geq-\frac{\tau}{(\theta-1)\left(\sigma-L_{h}\sqrt{\tau/((\theta-1)\gamma)}\right)^{2}}\|w-w^{\prime}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Thus, the desired inequality holds with $\rho:=\frac{\tau}{(\theta-1)\left(\sigma-L_{h}\sqrt{\tau/((\theta-1)\gamma)}\right)^{2}}$ .
Therefore, $\Phi:=G+T$ is globally $\rho$ -co-hypomonotone.
Step 5. Finally, we prove (iii).
By Minkowski’s inequality, the $\frac{1}{\theta-1}$ -Lipschitz continuity of $\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}$ , and the average smoothness of $f$ , for $\tilde{L}_{f}:=L_{f}+\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}$ , we can show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\nabla f_{i}(z)-\nabla f_{i}(z^{\prime})+B(y-y^{\prime})-\tau(\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z)-\nabla\tilde{\mathcal{R}}_{\lambda,\theta}(z^{\prime}))\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}{\!\!\!}\leq\tilde{L}_{f}\|z-z^{\prime}\|+\|B\|\|y-y^{\prime}\|.\end{array}$
Arguing as in Step 3 gives
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}x^{\prime}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x-x^{\prime}\|^{2},\end{array}$
(109)
where $L=\frac{\tilde{L}_{f}+L_{h}+\sqrt{(\tilde{L}_{f}-L_{h})^{2}+4\|B\|^{2}}}{2}$ .
We next bound $\|x-x^{\prime}\|$ by $\|w-w^{\prime}\|$ .
It is easy to show that
$\nabla h(y)-\nabla h(y^{\prime})=Q(y-y^{\prime})$ ,
where $Q:=\int_{0}^{1}\nabla^{2}h(y^{\prime}+t(y-y^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}t$ satisfies $\gamma\mathbb{I}\preceq Q\preceq L_{h}\mathbb{I}$ .
Thus, from the second block of (107), we get
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}y-y^{\prime}=Q^{-1}\big([w-w^{\prime}]_{y}+B^{\top}(z-z^{\prime})\big),\qquad\frac{1}{L_{h}}\mathbb{I}\preceq Q^{-1}\preceq\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathbb{I}.\end{array}$
(110)
Substituting this relation into the first block of (107), we can derive that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle[w-w^{\prime}]_{z}-BQ^{-1}[w-w^{\prime}]_{y},z-z^{\prime}\rangle&\geq&\frac{1}{L_{h}}\|B^{\top}(z-z^{\prime})\|^{2}-\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\|z-z^{\prime}\|^{2}\geq\big(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{L_{h}}-\frac{\tau}{\theta-1}\big)\|z-z^{\prime}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Since $\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)>\tau L_{h}$ , the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (110) yield
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|z-z^{\prime}\|&\leq&\frac{L_{h}(\theta-1)\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2}}}{\gamma\left[\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right]}\|w-w^{\prime}\|.\end{array}$
Using (110) again, we also have
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|y-y^{\prime}\|&\leq&\frac{\gamma\left[\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right]+L_{h}(\theta-1)\|B\|\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2}}}{\gamma^{2}\left[\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right]}\|w-w^{\prime}\|.\end{array}$
Therefore, combining the last two estimates, we finally arrive at
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|x-x^{\prime}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{L_{h}^{2}(\theta-1)^{2}(\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2})+\left[\gamma\left(\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right)+L_{h}(\theta-1)\|B\|\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2}}\right]^{2}}{\gamma^{2}\left[\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right]^{2}}\|w-w^{\prime}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Combining this estimate with (109), we obtain
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}x^{\prime}\|^{2}\leq\tilde{L}^{2}\|w-w^{\prime}\|^{2}\leq(1+\tilde{L}^{2})\|w-w^{\prime}\|^{2},\end{array}$
where $\hat{L}^{2}:=\frac{L^{2}\left\{\gamma^{2}L_{h}^{2}(\theta-1)^{2}\left(\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2}\right)+\left[\gamma\left(\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right)+L_{h}(\theta-1)\|B\|\sqrt{\gamma^{2}+\|B\|^{2}}\right]^{2}\right\}}{\gamma^{4}\left[\sigma^{2}(\theta-1)-\tau L_{h}\right]^{2}}$ .
Finally, by (ii), for any $\kappa\geq 0$ , it is obvious to show that
$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w-w^{\prime},x-x^{\prime}\rangle&\geq&-(1+\kappa)\rho\|w-w^{\prime}\|^{2}+\frac{\kappa\rho}{1+\hat{L}^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|G_{i}x-G_{i}x^{\prime}\|^{2}.\end{array}$
Let us denote $\rho_{n}:=(1+\kappa)\rho$ and $\rho_{c}:=\frac{\kappa\rho}{1+\hat{L}^{2}}$ .
Since $\rho_{n}-\rho_{c}=\rho\big(1+\frac{\kappa\hat{L}^{2}}{1+\hat{L}^{2}}\big)>0$ , $\Phi$ satisfies Assumption 1 with $\rho_{n}>\rho_{c}\geq 0$ .
$\square$

Instructions for reporting errors
We are continuing to improve HTML versions of papers, and your feedback helps enhance accessibility and mobile
support. To report errors in the HTML that will help us improve conversion and rendering, choose any of the
methods listed below:
Click the "Report Issue" (
) button, located in the page header.
Tip: You can select the relevant text first, to include it in your report.
Our team has already identified the following issues. We appreciate your time reviewing and reporting rendering errors we
may not have found yet. Your efforts will help us improve the HTML versions for all readers, because disability
should not be a barrier to accessing research. Thank you for your continued support in championing open access for
all.
Have a free development cycle? Help support accessibility at arXiv! Our collaborators at LaTeXML maintain a list of packages that need conversion, and welcome developer contributions.
We gratefully acknowledge support from
our major funders,
member institutions, ,
and all contributors.
